============================================================\n   社区智慧养老服务与健康监测平台 · 适老化主题\n   设计要点：大字号（正文 18px+）、高对比度、按钮 ≥44px、\n            表格斑马纹、预警红色置顶、键盘可访问\n   ============================================================ */\n\n:root {\n    --font-base: 18px;\n    --font-sm: 17px;\n    --font-lg: 20px;\n\n    --color-primary: #14508c;\n    --color-primary-dark: #0e3c6b;\n    --color-primary-light: #e8f1fb;\n    --color-accent: #c62828;\n    --color-success: #1b7f3b;\n    --color-warning: #a86400;\n    --color-info: #0b5fa5;\n\n    --color-text: #1a1a1a;\n    --color-text-muted: #4a5561;\n    --color-border: #c9d5e2;\n    --color-bg: #f4f7fb;\n    --color-surface: #ffffff;\n\n    --sidebar-bg: #0f3557;\n    --sidebar-bg-hover: #1b4c78;\n    --sidebar-active: #2f7fc1;\n\n    --radius: 10px;\n    --shadow-sm: 0 1px 2px rgba(15, 53, 87, .12);\n    --shadow-md: 0 4px 14px rgba(15, 53, 87, .14);\n    --btn-min-height: 44px;\n}\n\n* { box-sizing: border-box; }\n\nhtml[data-font-size=\"large\"] { --font-base: 21px; --font-sm: 20px; --font-lg: 23px; }\nhtml[data-font-size=\"extra\"] { --font-base: 24px; --font-sm: 22px; --font-lg: 26px; }\n.font-size-control { min-height: 44px; max-width: 110px; font: inherit; }\nhtml[data-font-size=\"large\"] :is(input, select, textarea, .btn, .data-table) { font-size: 20px; }\nhtml[data-font-size=\"extra\"] :is(input, select, textarea, .btn, .data-table) { font-size: 22px; }\n@media (max-width: 600px) {\n    .app-topbar { flex-wrap: wrap; }\n    .user-box { flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 8px; }\n    .page-heading { min-width: 0; }\n}\n\nhtml { font-size: var(--font-base); }\n\nbody.app-body {\n    margin: 0;\n    font-family: \"Microsoft YaHei\", \"PingFang SC\", \"Hiragino Sans GB\", \"Source Han Sans SC\", sans-serif;\n    font-size: var(--font-base);\n    line-height: 1.65;\n    color: var(--color-text);\n    background: var(--color-bg);\n    -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;\n}\n\nh1, h2, h3, h4, h5 { color: var(--color-primary-dark); font-weight: 700; margin: 0 0 .6rem; }\nh1 { font-size: 30px; }\nh2 { font-size: 25px; }\nh3 { font-size: 21px; }\nh4 { font-size: 19px; }\np { margin: 0 0 .75rem; }\na { color: var(--color-primary); text-decoration: none; }\na:hover { text-decoration: underline; }\na:focus-visible, button:focus-visible, input:focus-visible, select:focus-visible,\ntextarea:focus-visible, .nav-item:focus-visible {\n    outline: 3px solid #f9a825;\n    outline-offset: 2px;\n}\n\n.skip-link {\n    position: absolute; left: -9999px; top: 0; z-index: 999;\n    background: #fff; color: var(--color-primary); padding: 10px 16px;\n}\n.skip-link:focus { left: 12px; top: 12px; }\n\n/* ---------------- 布局 ---------------- */\n.app-shell { display: flex; min-height: 100vh; }\n\n.app-sidebar {\n    width: 264px;\n    flex: 0 0 264px;\n    background: var(--sidebar-bg);\n    color: #eaf2fa;\n    display: flex;\n    flex-direction: column;\n    padding: 0 0 18px;\n    position: sticky;\n    top: 0;\n    height: 100vh;\n    overflow-y: auto;\n}\n\n.brand {\n    display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 12px;\n    padding: 20px 18px 18px;\n    border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, .16);\n}\n.brand-logo {\n    width: 46px; height: 46px; flex: 0 0 46px;\n    display: grid; place-items: center;\n    background: #2f7fc1; color: #fff;\n    border-radius: 12px; font-size: 24px; font-weight: 700;\n}\n.brand-name { font-size: 19px; font-weight: 700; color: #fff; line-height: 1.25; }\n.brand-sub { font-size: 14px; color: #b8d3ea; line-height: 1.3; }\n\n.app-nav { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 2px; padding: 12px 10px; }\n.nav-item {\n    display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 12px;\n    min-height: 48px; padding: 10px 14px;\n    color: #dbe9f6; border-radius: 8px;\n    font-size: 18px; font-weight: 600;\n}\n.nav-item:hover { background: var(--sidebar-bg-hover); color: #fff; text-decoration: none; }\n.nav-item.active { background: var(--sidebar-active); color: #fff; box-shadow: inset 4px 0 0 #ffd54f; }\n.nav-icon { width: 24px; text-align: center; font-size: 19px; }\n.nav-divider { height: 1px; margin: 10px 8px; background: rgba(255, 255, 255, .18); }\n.nav-group-label {\n    font-size: 14px; letter-spacing: .12em; color: #9dbdd8;\n    padding: 10px 16px 4px; text-transform: uppercase;\n}\n.sidebar-foot { margin-top: auto; padding: 14px 18px 0; font-size: 14px; color: #9dbdd8; }\n\n.app-main { flex: 1 1 auto; min-width: 0; display: flex; flex-direction: column; }\n\n.app-topbar {\n    display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 16px;\n    padding: 12px 26px;\n    background: var(--color-surface);\n    border-bottom: 2px solid var(--color-border);\n    position: sticky; top: 0; z-index: 20;\n}\n.page-heading { flex: 1 1 auto; min-width: 0; }\n.page-heading .breadcrumb { font-size: 15px; color: var(--color-text-muted); margin-bottom: 2px; }\n.page-heading h1 { font-size: 24px; margin: 0; line-height: 1.3; }\n\n.user-box { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 12px; font-size: 17px; }\n.user-name { font-weight: 700; }\n.role-badge {\n    display: inline-block; padding: 5px 12px; border-radius: 999px;\n    font-size: 15px; font-weight: 700; color: #fff; background: var(--color-primary);\n    white-space: nowrap;\n}\n.role-elder { background: #6a1b9a; }\n.role-nurse { background: #00695c; }\n.role-grid { background: #14508c; }\n.role-org_admin { background: #a04b00; }\n.role-street { background: #283593; }\n.role-super { background: #b71c1c; }\n\n.app-content { padding: 22px 26px 48px; max-width: 1560px; width: 100%; }\n\n.menu-toggle { display: none; }\n\n/* ---------------- 卡片 / 区块 ---------------- */\n.card {\n    background: var(--color-surface);\n    border: 1px solid var(--color-border);\n    border-radius: var(--radius);\n    box-shadow: var(--shadow-sm);\n    margin-bottom: 20px;\n    overflow: hidden;\n}\n.card-header {\n    display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; gap: 12px;\n    padding: 14px 20px;\n    background: #eef4fb;\n    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--color-border);\n    font-size: 19px; font-weight: 700; color: var(--color-primary-dark);\n}\n.card-header .sub { font-size: 15px; font-weight: 400; color: var(--color-text-muted); }\n.card-body { padding: 20px; }\n.card-body.tight { padding: 12px 16px; }\n.card-footer { padding: 12px 20px; background: #f7fafd; border-top: 1px solid var(--color-border); }\n\n.grid { display: grid; gap: 20px; }\n.grid-2 { grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr)); }\n.grid-3 { grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr)); }\n.grid-4 { grid-template-columns: repeat(4, minmax(0, 1fr)); }\n.grid-2-1 { grid-template-columns: 2fr 1fr; }\n\n/* 看板数字卡 */\n.stat-card {\n    display: block; background: var(--color-surface);\n    border: 1px solid var(--color-border);\n    border-left: 8px solid var(--color-primary);\n    border-radius: var(--radius);\n    padding: 18px 20px;\n    box-shadow: var(--shadow-sm);\n    color: inherit;\n}\n.stat-card:hover { text-decoration: none; box-shadow: var(--shadow-md); }\n.stat-label { font-size: 17px; color: var(--color-text-muted); font-weight: 600; }\n.stat-value { font-size: 38px; font-weight: 800; color: var(--color-primary-dark); line-height: 1.15; }\n.stat-hint { font-size: 15px; color: var(--color-text-muted); }\n.stat-card.danger { border-left-color: var(--color-accent); }\n.stat-card.danger .stat-value { color: var(--color-accent); }\n.stat-card.warn { border-left-color: #e08b00; }\n.stat-card.warn .stat-value { color: var(--color-warning); }\n.stat-card.ok { border-left-color: var(--color-success); }\n.stat-card.ok .stat-value { color: var(--color-success); }\n\n/* ---------------- 按钮（≥44px 触控友好） ---------------- */\n.btn {\n    display: inline-flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; gap: 6px;\n    min-height: var(--btn-min-height);\n    padding: 9px 20px;\n    font-size: 18px; font-weight: 700; font-family: inherit;\n    border: 2px solid transparent; border-radius: 8px;\n    cursor: pointer; text-align: center; text-decoration: none;\n    transition: background .15s, border-color .15s, color .15s;\n    white-space: nowrap;\n}\n.btn:hover { text-decoration: none; }\n.btn:disabled, .btn.disabled { opacity: .55; cursor: not-allowed; }\n.btn-primary { background: var(--color-primary); border-color: var(--color-primary); color: #fff; }\n.btn-primary:hover { background: var(--color-primary-dark); border-color: var(--color-primary-dark); color: #fff; }\n.btn-secondary { background: #fff; border-color: var(--color-primary); color: var(--color-primary); }\n.btn-secondary:hover { background: var(--color-primary-light); color: var(--color-primary-dark); }\n.btn-danger { background: var(--color-accent); border-color: var(--color-accent); color: #fff; }\n.btn-danger:hover { background: #9c1f1f; border-color: #9c1f1f; color: #fff; }\n.btn-success { background: var(--color-success); border-color: var(--color-success); color: #fff; }\n.btn-success:hover { background: #14632e; color: #fff; }\n.btn-warning { background: #ef8b00; border-color: #ef8b00; color: #fff; }\n.btn-warning:hover { background: #cf7800; color: #fff; }\n.btn-ghost { background: transparent; border-color: var(--color-border); color: var(--color-primary-dark); }\n.btn-ghost:hover { background: var(--color-primary-light); }\n.btn-sm { min-height: 38px; padding: 6px 14px; font-size: 16px; }\n.btn-block { width: 100%; }\n.btn-row { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 10px; align-items: center; }\n\n/* ---------------- 表单 ---------------- */\n.form-grid { display: grid; gap: 16px; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(260px, 1fr)); }\n.form-field { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 6px; }\n.form-field > label, .form-label { font-size: 17px; font-weight: 700; color: var(--color-primary-dark); }\n.form-field .hint { font-size: 15px; color: var(--color-text-muted); }\ninput[type=\"text\"], input[type=\"password\"], input[type=\"number\"], input[type=\"date\"],\ninput[type=\"datetime-local\"], input[type=\"search\"], select, textarea {\n    min-height: 46px;\n    padding: 9px 12px;\n    font-size: 18px; font-family: inherit;\n    color: var(--color-text);\n    background: #fff;\n    border: 2px solid var(--color-border);\n    border-radius: 8px;\n    width: 100%;\n}\ntextarea { min-height: 96px; resize: vertical; }\ninput:focus, select:focus, textarea:focus { border-color: var(--color-primary); box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba(47, 127, 193, .22); outline: none; }\ninput[type=\"checkbox\"], input[type=\"radio\"] { width: 22px; height: 22px; accent-color: var(--color-primary); }\n.filter-bar {\n    display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 12px; align-items: flex-end;\n    padding: 14px 16px; background: #f2f7fc; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--color-border);\n}\n.filter-bar .form-field { min-width: 168px; flex: 0 1 auto; }\n.filter-bar .form-field.grow { flex: 1 1 240px; }\n\n/* ---------------- 表格（斑马纹） ---------------- */\n.table-wrap { width: 100%; overflow-x: auto; }\ntable.data-table {\n    width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; font-size: 17px;\n}\ntable.data-table th, table.data-table td {\n    padding: 12px 14px; text-align: left; vertical-align: middle;\n    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--color-border);\n}\ntable.data-table thead th {\n    background: #e3edf7; color: var(--color-primary-dark);\n    font-size: 17px; font-weight: 700; white-space: nowrap;\n    position: sticky; top: 0;\n}\ntable.data-table tbody tr:nth-child(even) { background: #f5f9fd; }\ntable.data-table tbody tr:nth-child(odd) { background: #fff; }\ntable.data-table tbody tr:hover { background: #e8f1fb; }\ntable.data-table td.actions { white-space: nowrap; }\ntable.data-table td.num, table.data-table th.num { text-align: right; font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }\ntable.data-table tr.row-red { background: #fdeaea !important; box-shadow: inset 6px 0 0 #c62828; }\ntable.data-table tr.row-yellow { background: #fff8e6 !important; box-shadow: inset 6px 0 0 #ef8b00; }\ntable.data-table tr.row-blue { background: #eaf3fd !important; box-shadow: inset 6px 0 0 #1976d2; }\ntable.data-table tr.row-muted td { color: #7a8794; }\n\n/* ---------------- 徽标 / 状态 ---------------- */\n.badge {\n    display: inline-block; padding: 3px 11px; border-radius: 999px;\n    font-size: 15px; font-weight: 700; line-height: 1.6;\n    background: #e6edf5; color: #33475b; white-space: nowrap;\n}\n.badge-blue { background: #e0eefc; color: #0b4c8c; }\n.badge-yellow { background: #fff1cc; color: #8a5200; }\n.badge-red { background: #fbdcdc; color: #a11616; }\n.badge-green { background: #dcf3e4; color: #14632e; }\n.badge-gray { background: #e8eaed; color: #4a5561; }\n.badge-purple { background: #f0e4fb; color: #6a1b9a; }\n.badge-outline { background: transparent; border: 2px solid var(--color-border); }\n\n.tag-chip {\n    display: inline-block; padding: 4px 12px; margin: 0 6px 6px 0;\n    border-radius: 999px; background: #eaf3fd; color: #0b4c8c;\n    border: 1px solid #b9d5ef; font-size: 16px; font-weight: 700;\n}\n.tag-chip.warn { background: #fff1cc; color: #8a5200; border-color: #f0cd80; }\n.tag-chip.danger { background: #fbdcdc; color: #a11616; border-color: #f0b3b3; }\n\n/* ---------------- 进度条（派单因子） ---------------- */\n.factor { margin-bottom: 10px; }\n.factor-head { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; font-size: 16px; font-weight: 700; color: #33475b; }\n.progress { height: 16px; background: #e4ebf3; border-radius: 999px; overflow: hidden; }\n.progress > span { display: block; height: 100%; background: linear-gradient(90deg, #2f7fc1, #14508c); border-radius: 999px; }\n.progress.gold > span { background: linear-gradient(90deg, #f6b93b, #d98200); }\n\n/* ---------------- 空态 / 提示 ---------------- */\n.empty-state {\n    padding: 44px 20px; text-align: center; color: var(--color-text-muted);\n    font-size: 18px; background: #f7fafd; border-radius: var(--radius);\n}\n.empty-state .icon { font-size: 34px; display: block; margin-bottom: 8px; }\n.notice {\n    padding: 14px 18px; border-radius: var(--radius); font-size: 17px;\n    border-left: 8px solid var(--color-primary); background: var(--color-primary-light);\n    margin-bottom: 18px;\n}\n.notice.warn { border-left-color: #e08b00; background: #fff6e2; }\n.notice.danger { border-left-color: var(--color-accent); background: #fdeaea; }\n.notice.success { border-left-color: var(--color-success); background: #e8f7ee; }\n.demo-tip {\n    font-size: 15px; color: var(--color-text-muted);\n    background: #fbfcfe; border: 1px dashed var(--color-border);\n    border-radius: 8px; padding: 8px 12px; margin-top: 10px;\n}\n\n/* ---------------- 列表分页 ---------------- */\n.pagination { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 8px; align-items: center; justify-content: flex-end; padding: 14px 20px; }\n.pagination .page-link {\n    display: inline-flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;\n    min-width: 46px; min-height: 44px; padding: 6px 14px;\n    border: 2px solid var(--color-border); border-radius: 8px;\n    background: #fff; color: var(--color-primary); font-weight: 700; font-size: 17px;\n}\n.pagination .page-link:hover { background: var(--color-primary-light); text-decoration: none; }\n.pagination .page-link.active { background: var(--color-primary); border-color: var(--color-primary); color: #fff; }\n.pagination .total { font-size: 16px; color: var(--color-text-muted); margin-left: auto; }\n\n/* ---------------- 登录页 ---------------- */\n.login-page {\n    min-height: 100vh; display: grid; place-items: center;\n    background: linear-gradient(135deg, #0f3557 0%, #1b5e9e 55%, #2f7fc1 100%);\n    padding: 24px;\n}\n.login-card {\n    width: 100%; max-width: 520px; background: #fff;\n    border-radius: 16px; box-shadow: 0 18px 50px rgba(6, 26, 44, .4);\n    padding: 38px 40px 34px;\n}\n.login-brand { text-align: center; margin-bottom: 26px; }\n.login-brand .logo {\n    width: 68px; height: 68px; margin: 0 auto 12px; border-radius: 18px;\n    background: var(--color-primary); color: #fff; display: grid; place-items: center;\n    font-size: 34px; font-weight: 800;\n}\n.login-brand h1 { font-size: 25px; margin-bottom: 6px; }\n.login-brand p { color: var(--color-text-muted); font-size: 16px; margin: 0; }\n.login-form .form-field { margin-bottom: 18px; }\n.login-form input { min-height: 52px; font-size: 19px; }\n.login-form .btn { min-height: 52px; font-size: 20px; }\n.login-error {\n    background: #fdeaea; border-left: 8px solid var(--color-accent);\n    color: #8f1414; padding: 12px 16px; border-radius: 8px; margin-bottom: 18px; font-weight: 700;\n}\n.demo-accounts {\n    margin-top: 24px; padding-top: 18px; border-top: 1px dashed var(--color-border);\n    font-size: 15px; color: var(--color-text-muted);\n}\n.demo-accounts table { width: 100%; font-size: 15px; }\n.demo-accounts td { padding: 3px 6px; }\n.demo-accounts .pwd { font-weight: 700; color: var(--color-primary-dark); }\n\n/* ---------------- 状态条 / 时间线 ---------------- */\n.timeline { list-style: none; margin: 0; padding: 0; }\n.timeline li { position: relative; padding: 0 0 16px 26px; border-left: 3px solid unavailable”；派单业务已完成。改为无协议慢速、关键步骤显式阅读停顿，后续通过。\n+\n+以上失败保留在对应运行目录，均未冒充业务Bug或用户反馈。\n+\n+## 修改范围\n+\n+- `app/static/js/app.js`：公开toast、通用动作防重入、加载及失败恢复。\n+- `app/templates/alerts/index.html`：处置失败保留输入和弹窗。\n+- `scripts/verify_browser.py`：隔离数据库、Edge真实流程、体验与注入验证、截图录像。\n+\n+## 验证证据\n+\n+- 未修复基线：`reports/chuanzhi-browser/20261112-170748/result.json`。\n+- text(await tools.apply_patch("*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: D:\\Program Files (x86)\\巅峰\\python\\eldercare-platform\\reports\\chuanzhi-ai-recording\\20261112-173311\\制作与真实性说明.md\n+# AI协同记录复现录像说明\n+\n+本次录制使用Playwright驱动Edge，实际点击本地证据查看器，依次展示真实可见用户请求、助手回复、真实失败输出摘录、真实AI补丁、本轮当前文件及文件哈希，最后点击执行实际Ruff、JS语法及隔离HTTP冒烟。\n+\n+- 原始WebM：`video/page@5ed454f3ff44d8da260e8426e63b0c7e.webm`。\n+- ffmpeg实测时长123.20秒，1920×1080，25fps，VP9，无音轨。\n+- 播放过程目标122秒；浏览器建立和收尾增加少量帧，未通过伪造命令结果延长时长。\n+- 最终真实命令退出码均为0：Ruff约0.188秒，JS约0.078秒，verify_demo约7.703秒。\n+- `real-verification.json`保存本次stdout、stderr、退出码和实际耗时；`result.json`保存录像路径及总体结论。\n+- `cards.json`与`evidence-viewer.html`保存录制时展示的数据与页面，所有摘录包含来源位置、真实时间及原记录或文件哈希。\n+- 原始失败目录`20261112-172754`保留；该次verify_demo临时库清理WinError32导致退出1。复现分支修复自己启动的服务进程树关闭逻辑，经连续3次实测通过后，本次录制重新执行完整验收成功。\n+\n+这段是AI开发证据的交互式重放及实际检查，不是重新在线生成模块，不是参赛人手工输入Prompt的原始录像，不代表已满足三条独立完整Prompt链。展示失败反馈和代码状态供评审核对；是否满足主办方对“AI协同过程复现展示”的具体解释仍应以规则审核为准。\n+\n+建议与参赛人原功能演示拼接时统一1920×1080、30fps、H.264、AAC采样率44100Hz，并为本段加入静音AAC。旧功能段348.87秒，本段123.20秒，总体约472.07秒，在5至8分钟内；仍需以最终MP4实际探测为准。\n+\n+此前独立视频审阅确认旧版最终MP4可全片解码，7分52.39秒，1920×1080/30fps；原功能段存在音频（全片mean约-39.9dB、max约-0.8dB），并非完全无音轨。静态证据图曾发现Consolas中文哈希标签显示方框，根代理已修复；此交互HTML全部使用含中文字体的回退方案，截图检查未发现方框。\n*** End Patch"));
